# Palkeoramix decompiler. 

def storage:
  owner is address at storage 0
  nonce is mapping of uint256 at storage 1
  remainingRaw is mapping of uint256 at storage 2
  invalidatorForOrderRFQ is mapping of mapping of uint256 at storage 3

def SafeMath:
  def add(a, b):  # solidity >= 0.8
      if a > a + b:
          revert with Panic(17)  # If an arithmetic operation results in underflow or overflow outside of an unchecked { ... } block.
      return a + b
  def div(a, b):  # solidity >= 0.8
      if not b:
          revert with Panic(18)  # If you divide or modulo by zero (e.g. 5 / 0 or 23 % 0).
      return a / b
  def inc(a):  # solidity >= 0.8
      if a == -1:
          revert with Panic(17)  # If an arithmetic operation results in underflow or overflow outside of an unchecked { ... } block.
      return a + 1

def invalidatorForOrderRFQ(address maker, uint256 slot): # not payable
  require calldata.size - 4 >=′ 64
  require maker == address(maker)
  return invalidatorForOrderRFQ[maker][slot]

def nonce(address _param1): # not payable
  require calldata.size - 4 >=′ 32
  require _param1 == address(_param1)
  return nonce[_param1]

def remainingRaw(bytes32 orderHash): # not payable
  require calldata.size - 4 >=′ 32
  return remainingRaw[orderHash]

def owner(): # not payable
  return owner

def cancelOrderRFQ(uint256 orderInfo): # not payable
  require calldata.size - 4 >=′ 32

      revert with InvalidatedOrder()
  invalidatorForOrderRFQ[caller][uint64(orderInfo) / 256] = invalidatorForOrderRFQ[caller][uint64(orderInfo) / 256] | 1 << uint8(orderInfo)

def cancelOrderRFQ(uint256 orderInfo, uint256 additionalMask): # not payable

def remainingsRaw(bytes32[] orderHashes): # not payable
  require calldata.size - 4 >=′ 32
  require orderHashes <= 18446744073709551615
  require orderHashes + 35 <′ calldata.size
  if orderHashes.length > 18446744073709551615:
      revert with Panic(65)  # If you allocate too much memory or create an array that is too large.
  if 32 * orderHashes.length + 160 < 128 or 32 * orderHashes.length + 160 > 18446744073709551615:
      revert with Panic(65)  # If you allocate too much memory or create an array that is too large.
  require orderHashes + 32 * orderHashes.length + 36 <= calldata.size
  s = 160
  idx = orderHashes + 36
  while idx < orderHashes + 32 * orderHashes.length + 36:
      mem[s] = cd[idx]
      s = s + 32
      idx = idx + 32
      continue
  if orderHashes.length > 18446744073709551615:
      revert with Panic(65)  # If you allocate too much memory or create an array that is too large.
  mem[32 * orderHashes.length + 160] = orderHashes.length
  if orderHashes.length:
      mem[32 * orderHashes.length + 192 len 32 * orderHashes.length] = call.data[calldata.size len 32 * orderHashes.length]
  _12 = orderHashes.length
  idx = 0
  while idx < _12:
      if idx >= orderHashes.length:
          revert with Panic(50)  # If you access an array, bytesN or an array slice at an out-of-bounds or negative index (i.e. x[i] where i >= x.length or i < 0).
      mem[0] = mem[32 * idx + 160]
      mem[32] = 2
      if idx >= orderHashes.length:
          revert with Panic(50)  # If you access an array, bytesN or an array slice at an out-of-bounds or negative index (i.e. x[i] where i >= x.length or i < 0).
      mem[32 * idx + 32 * orderHashes.length + 192] = remainingRaw[concat(mem[32 * idx + 160])]
      _12 = orderHashes.length
      idx = SafeMath.inc(idx)
      continue
  return Array(len=orderHashes.length, data=mem[32 * orderHashes.length + 192 len 32 * orderHashes.length])

def swap(address executor, (address,address,address,address,uint256,uint256,uint256) desc, bytes permit, bytes data) payable: 
  require calldata.size - 4 >=′ 320
  require executor == address(executor)
  require calldata.size - 36 >=′ 224
  require permit <= 18446744073709551615
  require permit + 35 <′ calldata.size
  require permit.length <= 18446744073709551615
  require permit + permit.length + 36 <= calldata.size
  require data <= 18446744073709551615
  require data + 35 <′ calldata.size
  require data.length <= 18446744073709551615
  require data + data.length + 36 <= calldata.size
  if not desc.minReturnAmount:
      revert with ZeroMinReturn()
  require desc.srcToken == address(desc.srcToken)
  require desc.dstToken == address(desc.dstToken)
  _1 = not desc.srcToken or 0xeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeee == desc.srcToken
  if not Mask(1, 1, desc.flags):
      _2 = _1 and desc.amount
      if _2 != call.value:
          revert with InvalidMsgValue()
  else:
      _3 = _1 and desc.amount
      if call.value <= _3:
          revert with InvalidMsgValue()
  if not _1:
      if permit.length:
          if permit.length != 224:
              if permit.length != 256:
                  revert with SafePermitBadLength()
              _4 = 0x8fcbaf0c00000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000
          else:
              _4 = 0xd505accf00000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000
          call desc.srcToken with:
             funct Bytes(4, _4 >> 224)
               gas gas_remaining
              args permit[all]
          mem[0] = ext_call.return_data[0]
          if not ext_call.success:
              _7 = 0
          else:
              if not return_data.size:
                  _6 = ext_code.size(desc.srcToken) > 0
              else:
                  _6 = return_data.size > 31 and ext_call.return_data[0] == 1
              _7 = _6
          if not _7:
              revert with ext_call.return_data[0 len return_data.size]
      require desc.srcReceiver == address(desc.srcReceiver)
      call desc.srcToken.transferFrom(address sender, address recipient, uint256 amount) with:
           gas gas_remaining
